select distinct ICD9Code from CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 where ICD9Code in ('398.91','428.0','428.1','428.20','428.21','428.22','428.23','428.30',
						'428.31','428.32','428.33','428.40','428.41','428.42','428.43','428.9')

select distinct ICD10Code from CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 where ICD10Code in ('I50.00','I50.1','I50.20','I50.30','I50.40','I50.9',
									'I11.0','I13.0','I13.2','I25.5')-- 150.00 is not available

/*ICD9 Codes*/

into #tempdiabetesHF

from Dflt.Diabetes_AFfinal as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('398.91','428.0','428.1','428.20','428.21','428.22','428.23','428.30',
						'428.31','428.32','428.33','428.40','428.41','428.42','428.43','428.9')

select distinct scrssn from #tempdiabetesHF --624,481

select distinct scrssn from #tempdiabetesHF where comorbidity_Dischargedatetime is not Null --494,304
 
select scrssn , min (comorbidity_dischargedatetime) as FirstHF_DischargeDatetime
into #tempHFDate
from #tempdiabetesHF group by scrssn

select distinct scrssn from #tempHFDate where FirstHF_DischargeDatetime < '2022-12-31 00:00:00' --483,605

select distinct a.PatientSID,a.ScrSSN, a.Sta3n, a.DischargeDateTime, a.comorbidity_Dischargedatetime, a.comorbidity_ICD9Code,b.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime
into #temp_Diabetes_HF
from #tempdiabetesHF as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempHFDate as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn = b.scrssn and a.comorbidity_Dischargedatetime= b.FirstHF_DischargeDatetime
and FirstHF_DischargeDatetime < '2022-12-31 00:00:00'

select distinct scrssn from #temp_Diabetes_HF where FirstHF_DischargeDatetime < '2022-12-31 00:00:00' --483,624

/* LVEF 40 and below */
 
select distinct a.*, b.Low_Value, b.ValueDateTime, b.High_Value

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF

from #temp_Diabetes_HF as a with (NOLOCK) inner join 
Src.VINCI_TIU_NLP_LVEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where b.Low_Value between 10 and 40 and  b.ValueDateTime between dateadd(year, -1, a.FirstHF_DischargeDatetime) and dateadd(month, 6, a.FirstHF_DischargeDatetime)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF -- 251,639
select top 1000* from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF
alter table Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF rebuild with (data_compression=page)

/* Select patients on Loop Diuretics */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.DischargeDateTime, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, b.FillDateTime, b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply, b.Qty 

into #tempmedloop

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%furosemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%torsemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumetanide%' or
		LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Ethacrynic acid%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lasix%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumex%' 
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.DischargeDateTime, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime,b.FillDateTime, b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply, b.Qty

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%furosemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%torsemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumetanide%' or
		LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Ethacrynic acid%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lasix%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumex%' 

select *

into #Diabetes_HFrEF_loop

from #tempmedLoop where DaysSupply >= 90 
and FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, FirstHF_Dischargedatetime) and dateadd(month, 6, FirstHF_Dischargedatetime)

select distinct scrssn from #Diabetes_HFrEF_loop --132,270

/* BNP  */

select distinct labchemtestname 
from CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest
where 
(labchemtestname like '%BNP%' or  
	labchemtestname  like '%brain natriuretic peptide%' or 
	labchemtestname  like '%natriu%pept%') and
	LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%' AND	LabChemTestName not like '%pro%'

select distinct b.PatientSID, b.ScrSSN, b.DischargeDateTime, b.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue, c.LabChemTestName

into #Diabetes_BNP

from Src.Chem_LabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
where 
 (labchemtestname like '%BNP%' or  
	labchemtestname  like '%brain natriuretic peptide%' or 
	labchemtestname  like '%natriu%pept%') and
	LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%' AND	LabChemTestName not like '%pro%'

union all

select distinct b.PatientSID, b.ScrSSN, b.DischargeDateTime,b.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime,  a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue, c.LabChemTestName

from Src.Chem_LabChem_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
where 
 (labchemtestname like '%BNP%' or  
	labchemtestname  like '%brain natriuretic peptide%' or 
	labchemtestname  like '%natriu%pept%') and
	LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%' AND	LabChemTestName not like '%pro%'

union all

select distinct b.PatientSID, b.ScrSSN, b.DischargeDateTime,b.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue, c.LabChemTestName

from Src.Chem_PatientLabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
where 
 (labchemtestname like '%BNP%' or  
	labchemtestname  like '%brain natriuretic peptide%' or 
	labchemtestname  like '%natriu%pept%') and
	LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%' AND	LabChemTestName not like '%pro%'

into #Diabetes_HErEFBNP

from #Diabetes_BNP where LabChemResultNumericValue > 100

/* PBNP */

select distinct b.PatientSID, b.ScrSSN, b.DischargeDateTime, b.FirstHF_dischargedatetime,  a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue, c.LabChemTestName

into #Diabetes_PBNP

from Src.Chem_LabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE 
  (--labchemtestname like '%BNP%' or 
   labchemtestname like '%terminal%pep%' or
	   labchemtestname  like '%proBNP%' or 
	   labchemtestname  like '%pro[- /_]BNP%' or 
 	   labchemtestname  like '%pro brain natriuretic peptide%' ) and
 	   --labchemtestname  like '%natriu%pept%'
	   labChemTestName not like '%ratio%'
union all

select distinct  b.PatientSID, b.ScrSSN, b.DischargeDateTime, b.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue, c.LabChemTestName

from Src.Chem_LabChem_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE 
  (--labchemtestname like '%BNP%' or 
   labchemtestname like '%terminal%pep%' or
	   labchemtestname  like '%proBNP%' or 
	   labchemtestname  like '%pro[- /_]BNP%' or 
 	   labchemtestname  like '%pro brain natriuretic peptide%' ) and
 	   --labchemtestname  like '%natriu%pept%'
	   labChemTestName not like '%ratio%'

union all

select distinct  b.PatientSID, b.ScrSSN, b.DischargeDateTime,b.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue, c.LabChemTestName

from Src.Chem_PatientLabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE 
  (--labchemtestname like '%BNP%' or 
   labchemtestname like '%terminal%pep%' or
	   labchemtestname  like '%proBNP%' or 
	   labchemtestname  like '%pro[- /_]BNP%' or 
 	   labchemtestname  like '%pro brain natriuretic peptide%' ) and 
 	   --labchemtestname  like '%natriu%pept%'
	   labChemTestName not like '%ratio%'

/*Combining loop and BNP/PBNP cohort using date filter */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.DischargeDateTime, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, a.FillDateTime

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_Loop_BNP_PBNP

from #Diabetes_HFrEF_loop as a where DaysSupply >=90 
and FillDateTime between dateadd(DAY, -180,  a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime) and dateadd(month, 6,  a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime)
union all

select a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.DischargeDateTime,  a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime,a.LabChemCompleteDateTime

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_BNP_PBNP as a
where LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(DAY, -180,  a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime) and dateadd(month, 6,  a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_Loop_BNP_PBNP--188,184

alter table Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_Loop_BNP_PBNP rebuild with (data_compression=page)

/* GLP1 Analogue */

select distinct LocalDrugNameWithDose from CDWWork.Dim.LocalDrug where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, b.FillDateTime as GLP1FillTime , b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply

into #tempglp1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_Loop_BNP_PBNP as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, b.FillDateTime as GLP1FillTime , b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_Loop_BNP_PBNP as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'

select distinct scrssn from #tempglp1 --14,379 ; repeated on 6/23/26: n = 16,818, didn't use date filter yet

/* 6/23/2026: Attempt to extract first fill time and 3rd fill time */
select top 100 * from #tempglp1 --familiarizing myself with the data

select distinct scrssn, glp1filltime into #tempfilltime from #tempglp1 --this captures distinct records and removes duplicates
--extract 3rd row

select distinct scrssn, GLP1FillTime as ThirdGLP1FillTime
into #tempThirdFillTime1
from (
	select  distinct scrssn, GLP1FillTime, row_number() over (
									partition by scrssn order by GLP1FillTime
									) as rn
	from #tempfilltime -- used this table here which has distinct records instead of the master table which has all records, this solved the duplicates issue
	) x
where rn = 3

select * from #tempThirdFillTime1 --14,588

--join back to main file
select distinct a.scrssn, min(a.GLP1FillTime) as FirstGLP1FillTime, b.ThirdGLP1FillTime
from #tempglp1 a 
left join #tempThirdFillTime1 b
	on a.scrssn=b.scrssn
group by a.scrssn, b.ThirdGLP1FillTime

select distinct a.*, c.FirstGLP1Filltime, b.ThirdGLP1FillTime
into #tempfirsttry
from #tempglp1 a
left join (
	select scrssn, min(GLP1FillTime) as FirstGLP1FillTime
	from #tempglp1
	group by scrssn ) c
	on a.scrssn=c.scrssn
left join #tempThirdFillTime1 b
on a.scrssn=b.scrssn
order by scrssn, GLP1FillTime --ordering only to confirm that code was executed properly

select distinct scrssn from #tempfirsttry where ThirdGLP1FillTime is not null

-- problem: there are many duplicate GLP1FillTime per scrssn, and the row_number() does not account for duplicates, so there are many instances where it extracts the first or second row rather than the third
-- trying again

select distinct scrssn, GLP1FillTime as ThirdGLP1FillTime -- added 'distinct' here
into #tempThirdFillTime2
from (
	select scrssn, GLP1FillTime, dense_rank() over(
									partition by scrssn order by GLP1FillTime
								) as fill_rank
	from #tempglp1
) x
where fill_rank=3

select * from #tempThirdFillTime2 -- this still had some duplicates so added distinct to the select statement above

--join again as above
select a.*, c.FirstGLP1FillTime, b.ThirdGLP1FillTime
into #tempFirstAndThirdFillTime
from #tempglp1 a
left join (
	select scrssn, min(GLP1FillTime) as FirstGLP1FillTime
	from #tempglp1
	group by scrssn) c
	on a.scrssn=c.scrssn
left join #tempThirdFillTime2 b
on a.scrssn=b.scrssn
order by scrssn, GLP1FillTime

--I think it worked this time
select distinct scrssn from #tempFirstAndThirdFillTime --16,818
select distinct scrssn from #tempFirstAndThirdFillTime where ThirdGLP1FillTime is not null --14,585 --> so presumably 2,233 were excluded for not having 3 refills

/*END OF EXPERIMENTATION 6/23/2026*/

/* GLP1 */
/*BMI*/

/* Select height and weight values from vital signs view to calculate bmi */
drop table #tempDiabetesVitals
select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into #tempDiabetesVitals

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.GLP1FillTime)) AND
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime))AND 
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.SGLT2FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.SGLT2FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime <= b.GLP1FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.SGLT2FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.SGLT2FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime <= b.GLP1FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')

select distinct scrssn from #tempDiabetesVitals

/* Seperate weight records into a table */
select * 

into #tempweight

from #tempDiabetesVitals where vitaltype = 'WEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempweight

select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempweight1
from #tempweight group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempweightfinal
from #tempweight as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempweight1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightfinal --1,458

/* Seperate height records into a table */
select *  

into #tempheight

from #tempDiabetesVitals where vitaltype = 'HEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempheight

select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempheight1
from #tempheight group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempheightfinal
from #tempheight as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempheight1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightfinal --1,475

/* Combine data from table weight and height to obtain the bmi for individual patient */
drop table  Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI

select distinct a.patientsid, a.ScrSSN, cast(a.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as HeightTime, a.VitalResultNumeric as heightresult, 
cast(b.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as WeightTime,b.VitalResultNumeric as weightresult, ((b.VitalResultNumeric*703)/(a.VitalResultNumeric*a.VitalResultNumeric)) as bmi

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI

from #tempheightfinal as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempweightfinal as b with (NOLOCK) on a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN
--where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime=b.VitalSignTakenDateTime --a.SGLT2FillTime=b.SGLT2FillTime and

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI --1457

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI where bmi < 30 --236

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI where bmi between 30 and 35 --357

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI where bmi between 35 and 40 --386

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI where bmi > 40 --478

/* Baseline Measurements for GLP1 Cohort */
/* Demographics */
/* Age */
drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Age

select a.ScrSSN, a.glp1Filltime, b.DOB,b.SEX
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_DOB
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.VitalStatus_Mini as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn

select *, datediff( year, DOB, glp1Filltime) as age
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Age
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_DOB

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Age where SEX ='F' --44

/* Date of Death */

select distinct a.ScrSSN,  b.MPI_DOD
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_DOD
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.SDAF_DAFMaster as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.MPI_scrssn
where b.MPI_DOD is not null

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_DOD --525

drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_DOD 

/* Race GLP1 */

select a.*,  b.Race
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Race
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.PatSub_PatientRace as b with (NOLOCK) on a.patientsid= b.patientsid

select distinct scrssn from  Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Race --1470

/* Hypertension */

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime as comorbidity_Dischargedatetime, c.ICD9Code as comorbidity_ICD9Code
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Hypertension

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('401.1','401.0','401.9','402.00','402.01','402.90','402.91','403.00','403.01','403.10','403.11','403.90','403.91','404.00','404.01','404.02','404.03','404.10',
					'404.11','404.12','404.13','404.90','404.91','404.92','404.93','405.01','405.09','405.11','405.99','642.00','642.01','642.02','642.03','642.04','642.10','642.11',
					'642.12','642.13','642.14','642.20','642.21','642.22','642.23','642.24','642.70','642.71','642.72','642.73','642.74','642.90','642.91','642.92','642.93','642.94')
					and b.DischargeDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)
union all

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD9Code

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Hypertension --905

/* Chronic Kidney Disease */
/*Making sure the ICD9 and ICD10 codes relevant to Kidney Disease are present in the Dimension table under CDWWork*/

select distinct ICD9Code from CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 where ICD9Code in ('403.01','403.11','403.91','404.02','404.03','404.12','404.92','404.93','585.5','585.6','V42.0','V45.11',
													'V56.0','V56.1','V56.2','V56.8', '585.3')

select distinct ICD10Code from CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 where ICD10Code in ('I12.0','I13.11','I13.2','N18.5','N18.6','Z94.0','Z99.2','N18.3') or ICD10Code like 'Z49.%'

Src.Inpat_InpatientFeeDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('V45.01','V45.02')
					and b.DischargeDateTime <= a.GLP1FillTime
union all

																	'I23.0','I23.1','I23.2','I23.3','I23.4','I23.5','I23.6','I23.7','I23.8','I24.0','I24.1','I24.8')

select distinct ICD9Code from CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 where ICD9Code in ('410.00','410.01','410.02','410.10','410.11','410.12','410.30','410.31','410.32','410.20','410.21','410.22',
														'410.40','410.41','410.42','410.50','410.51','410.52','410.60','410.61','410.62','410.80','410.81','410.82',
														'410.90','410.91','410.92','410.70','410.71','410.72','429.79','411.81','411.0','411.89')

select distinct a.patientsid, a.scrssn, a.GLP1FillTime, b.DischargeDateTime as comorbidity_Dischargedatetime, c.ICD9Code as comorbidity_ICD9Code
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_MI

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('410.00','410.01','410.02','410.10','410.11','410.12','410.30','410.31','410.32','410.20','410.21','410.22',
														'410.40','410.41','410.42','410.50','410.51','410.52','410.60','410.61','410.62','410.80','410.81','410.82',
														'410.90','410.91','410.92','410.70','410.71','410.72','429.79','411.81','411.0','411.89')
					and b.DischargeDateTime <= a.GLP1FillTime
union all

/*Ablation*/
/*Making sure the CPT codes relevant to Ablation are present in the Dimension table under CDWWork*/

select distinct CPTCode from CDWWork.Dim.CPT where CPTCode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')

select distinct a.*

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Ablation

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientCPTProcedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on c.CPTSID=b.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.DischargeDateTime <= a.GLP1Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Outpat_Vprocedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on c.CPTSID=b.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.EventDateTime <= a.GLP1Filltime
union all 

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Outpat_VProcedure_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on c.CPTSID=b.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.EventDateTime <= a.GLP1Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Surg_SurgeryPrincipalAssociatedProcedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on b.OtherProcedureCPTSID=c.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266') 
and b.SurgeryDateTime <= a.GLP1Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join

where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.SurgeryDateTime <= a.GLP1Filltime
union all

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join

and b.visitdatetime <= a.GLP1Filltime
union all

union all

/* Hematocrit */
/* Check for the labchemtestname in the dimension table */
select distinct labchemtestname from CDWWork.Dim.Labchemtest 
WHERE (LabChemTestName like '%hematocrit%' OR 
	LabChemTestName like '%hem%crit%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%packed%cell%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%PCV%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%VPRC%' OR 
	LabChemTestName like '%EVF%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%erythrocy%vol%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%haemto%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%hct%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%crit%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%h%&%h%')
	AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%serotype%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%PCV [0-9]%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%HPCV%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%cryocrit%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%dna%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%hiv%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%critical%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%A.%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%homocys%' and

AND LabChemTestName not like '%HEALTH%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Hepatitis%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Naltrex%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Kilodalton%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Malt%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Specialty%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Salt%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%balt%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%alto%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%ralt%'

select distinct b.*, a.LabChemResultValue,c.LabChemTestName, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue

into #tempALT

from Src.Chem_LabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE
(LabChemTestName like '%ALT%' OR 
LabChemTestName like '%alanine%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%aminotransferase%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%SGPT%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%SGOT%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%transaminase%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%GPT%' ) 
AND LabChemTestName not like '%ALTER%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%HEALTH%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Hepatitis%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Naltrex%'

AND LabChemTestName not like '%alto%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%ralt%'

union all

/* Blood Pressure */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric, a.systolic, a.diastolic,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BP

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.GLP1FillTime)) AND
c.VitalType in ('%Blood pressure%')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,a.systolic, a.diastolic,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime))AND 
c.VitalType like ('%Blood pressure%')

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BP --637

/* Total Number of HF hospitalization prior to GLP1 initiation */
drop table Dflt.HFrEF_GLP1_PriorHFHospi

select distinct a.*, b.AdmitDateTime

into Dflt.HFrEF_GLP1_PriorHFHospi

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('398.91','428.0','428.1','428.20','428.21','428.22','428.23','428.30',
						'428.31','428.32','428.33','428.40','428.41','428.42','428.43','428.9')
						and b.AdmitDateTime < a.GLP1FillTime
					and b.AdmitDateTime between dateadd(year, -1, a.GLP1FillTime) and a.GLP1FillTime

union all

select distinct a.*, b.AdmitDateTime

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('I50.00','I50.1','I50.20','I50.30','I50.40','I50.9',
									'I11.0','I13.0','I13.2','I25.5')
									and b.AdmitDateTime < a.GLP1FillTime
and b.AdmitDateTime between dateadd(year, -1, a.GLP1FillTime) and a.GLP1FillTime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.HFrEF_GLP1_PriorHFHospi --106, 333 , 355

Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%furosemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%torsemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumetanide%' or
		LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Ethacrynic acid%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lasix%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumex%'

select distinct *

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_LoopDiuretic

from #tempMedicationsloop 

where FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(month, 12, GLP1FillTime) and FillNumber > '1'

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_LoopDiuretic --1281

/* All HF hospitalization after GLP1 initiation */

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime,c.ICD9Code,b.AdmitDiagnosis
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.HFrEF_GLP1_HFHospitalization

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('398.91','428.0','428.1','428.20','428.21','428.22','428.23','428.30',
						'428.31','428.32','428.33','428.40','428.41','428.42','428.43','428.9')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.GLP1FillTime
union all

/*ICD10 Codes*/
select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10Code, b.AdmitDiagnosis

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('I50.00','I50.1','I50.20','I50.30','I50.40','I50.9',
									'I11.0','I13.0','I13.2','I25.5')
and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.GLP1FillTime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.HFrEF_GLP1_HFHospitalization --307

/*BMI*/
/* Select height and weight values from vital signs view to calculate bmi */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into #tempDiabetesVitalsGLP1

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(month, 3, b.GLP1FillTime) AND
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(month, 3, b.GLP1FillTime) AND 
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime >= b.GLP1FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime >= b.GLP1FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')

select distinct scrssn from #tempDiabetesVitalsGLP1--1443

/* Seperate weight records into a table */
select * 

into #tempweightGLP1After

from #tempDiabetesVitalsGLP1 where vitaltype = 'WEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightGLP1After --1428

/* Seperate height records into a table */
select *  

into #tempheightGLP1After

from #tempDiabetesVitalsGLP1 where vitaltype = 'HEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightGLP1After

/* Combine data from table weight and height to obtain the bmi for individual patient */

select distinct a.patientsid, a.ScrSSN, cast(a.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as HeightTime, a.VitalResultNumeric as heightresult, 
cast(b.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as WeightTime,b.VitalResultNumeric as weightresult, ((b.VitalResultNumeric*703)/(a.VitalResultNumeric*a.VitalResultNumeric)) as bmi

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI_After

from #tempheightGLP1After as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempweightGLP1After as b with (NOLOCK) on a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime=b.VitalSignTakenDateTime 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI_After --1247

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI_After where bmi between 30 and 35 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI_After where bmi between 35 and 40 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI_After where bmi > 40

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_BMI_After where bmi < 30 

/* Creatinine */

select *
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_CreatAfter
from #tempCreatinine
where LabChemCompleteDateTime > GLP1FillTime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_CreatAfter --1,458

/* Hematocrit */

select *
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_hematoAfter
from #tempHematocrit
where LabChemCompleteDateTime > GLP1FillTime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_hematoAfter --1450

drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_hematoAfter

/* Hemoglobin */

select *
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_hemoAfter
from #tempHemoglobin
where LabChemCompleteDateTime >  GLP1FillTime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_hemoAfter --1450

drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_hemoAfter

/* Atrial Fibrillation */

select distinct a.PatientSID,a.ScrSSN, a.GLP1FillTime, b.DischargeDateTime as comorbidity_Dischargedatetime, c.ICD9Code as comorbidity_ICD9Code, b.AdmitDiagnosis
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_AFAfter

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('427.31')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.GLP1FillTime
union all


/*ICD10 Codes*/
select distinct a.PatientSID,a.ScrSSN, a.GLP1FillTime, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10Code, b.AdmitDiagnosis

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%' 
union all

select distinct a.*, b.LocalDrugNameWithDose, b.FillDateTime, b.DrugNameWithoutDose

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'

select distinct *

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Drugdose 

from #tempglpdose

where GLP1Filltime = FillDateTime and GLP1Filltime >= '2015-01-01 00:00:00'

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Drugdose

select * from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Drugdose

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Drugdose 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' -- and GLP1Filltime >= '2014-01-01 00:00:00'

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Drugdose 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%'  --and GLP1Filltime >= '2014-01-01 00:00:00'

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Drugdose 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%'  --and GLP1Filltime >= '2014-01-01 00:00:00'

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Drugdose 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%'  --and GLP1Filltime >= '2014-01-01 00:00:00'

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_Drugdose 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%' --and GLP1Filltime >= '2014-01-01 00:00:00'

/***** DPP4 *****/

/* Baseline Measurements for DPP4GLP1 Cohort */
/* Demographics */
/* Age */

select a.ScrSSN, a.DPP4Filltime, b.DOB,b.SEX
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_DOB
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.VitalStatus_Mini as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn

select *, datediff( year, DOB, DPP4Filltime) as age
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_Age
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_DOB

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_Age where SEX ='F'

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_DOB

/* Date of Death */

select distinct a.ScrSSN,  b.MPI_DOD
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_DOD
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.SDAF_DAFMaster as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.MPI_scrssn
where b.MPI_DOD is not null

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_DOD --1242

/* Race GLP1 */

select a.*,  b.Race
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_Race
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.PatSub_PatientRace as b with (NOLOCK) on a.patientsid= b.patientsid

select distinct scrssn from  Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_Race --2339

select distinct CPTCode from CDWWork.Dim.CPT where CPTCode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')

select distinct a.*

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_Ablation

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientCPTProcedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on c.CPTSID=b.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.DischargeDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Outpat_Vprocedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on c.CPTSID=b.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.EventDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime
union all 

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Outpat_VProcedure_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on c.CPTSID=b.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.EventDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Surg_SurgeryPrincipalAssociatedProcedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on b.OtherProcedureCPTSID=c.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266') 
and b.SurgeryDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Surg_SurgeryProcedureDiagnosisCode as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalCPTSID=c.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.SurgeryDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Outpat_VProcedureCPTModifier as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on b.CPTSID=c.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.visitdatetime <= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Outpat_VProcedureDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on b.CPTSID=c.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.EventDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Outpat_WorkloadVProcedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on b.CPTSID=c.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.EventDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Outpat_WorkloadVProcedureCPTModifier as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on  b.CPTSID=c.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.VisitDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime

/* CAD */

'414.05','414.06','414.20','414.30','414.40','414.80','414.90','V45.81','V45.82')
					and b.DischargeDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime

/* CAD- PCI & CABG */

select a.*, b.SURGDATE

into #tempCABGDPP4GLP1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
src.VASQIP_nso_cardiac as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn
where b.cpt01 in ('33533','33534','33535','33536','33542','33545','33548')
and b.SURGDATE <= a.DPP4Filltime /* Check the dates */

select * from #tempCABGDPP4GLP1

select a.*, b.ProcedureDateTime

into #tempPCIDPP4GLP1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.CART_PCI as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where b.ProcedureDateTime <= a.DPP4Filltime /* Check the dates */

Select distinct patientsid, scrssn, DPP4Filltime, surgdate

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_ccp

from #tempCABGDPP4GLP1
union all

select distinct patientsid, scrssn, DPP4Filltime, ProcedureDateTime
from #tempPCIDPP4GLP1
union all 

select distinct patientsid, scrssn, DPP4Filltime, comorbidity_Dischargedatettime
from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_CAD

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_ccp --1717

/*BMI*/
/* Select height and weight values from vital signs view to calculate bmi */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.DPP4FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into #tempDiabetesVitals

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.DPP4FillTime)) AND
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.DPP4FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4FillTime))AND 
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.DPP4FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime <= b.DPP4FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.DPP4FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime <= b.DPP4FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')

select distinct scrssn from #tempDiabetesVitals -- 2343

/* Seperate weight records into a table */
select * 

into #tempweight

from #tempDiabetesVitals where vitaltype = 'WEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempweight

select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempweight1
from #tempweight group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempweightfinal
from #tempweight as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempweight1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightfinal --2321

/* Seperate height records into a table */
select *  

into #tempheight

from #tempDiabetesVitals where vitaltype = 'HEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempheight

select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempheight1
from #tempheight group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempheightfinal
from #tempheight as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempheight1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightfinal--2342

/* Combine data from table weight and height to obtain the bmi for individual patient */
drop table  Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI

select distinct a.patientsid, a.ScrSSN, cast(a.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as HeightTime, a.VitalResultNumeric as heightresult, 
cast(b.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as WeightTime,b.VitalResultNumeric as weightresult, ((b.VitalResultNumeric*703)/(a.VitalResultNumeric*a.VitalResultNumeric)) as bmi

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI

from #tempheightfinal as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempweightfinal as b with (NOLOCK) on a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI --2318

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI where bmi between 30 and 35 --656

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI where bmi between 35 and 40 --463

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI where bmi > 40 --368

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI where bmi < 30 --832

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI where bmi > 30 --1486

/* HBA1C */
/* Check for the labchemtestname in the dimension table */
select distinct labchemtestname from CDWWork.Dim.Labchemtest 
where LabChemTestName like '%A1C%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%ZZ%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'

/* Current VA phenomics library is updated and shows only %A1C% */

select b.*, a.LabChemResultValue, a.LabChemResultNumericValue, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime,c.LabChemTestName

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_hba1c

from Src.Chem_LabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
where c.LabChemTestName like '%A1C%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%ZZ%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'
and a.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime)                                
union all

select b.*, a.LabChemResultValue, a.LabChemResultNumericValue,a.LabChemCompleteDateTime,c.LabChemTestName

from Src.Chem_LabChem_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
where c.LabChemTestName like '%A1C%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%ZZ%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'
and a.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime)  
union all

select b.*, a.LabChemResultValue, a.LabChemResultNumericValue, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, c.LabChemTestName

from Src.Chem_PatientLabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
where c.LabChemTestName like '%A1C%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%ZZ%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'
and a.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, DPP4Filltime)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_hba1c --2080

'PANCREATIC POLYPEPTIDE~prior to 4/26/04','PANCREATITIS PANEL NGS(PG)', 'THALLIUM (ug/g Creat)')

/* Hematocrit */
/* Check for the labchemtestname in the dimension table */
select distinct labchemtestname from CDWWork.Dim.Labchemtest 
WHERE (LabChemTestName like '%hematocrit%' OR 
	LabChemTestName like '%hem%crit%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%packed%cell%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%PCV%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%VPRC%' OR 
	LabChemTestName like '%EVF%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%erythrocy%vol%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%haemto%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%hct%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%crit%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%h%&%h%')
	AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%serotype%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%PCV [0-9]%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%HPCV%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%cryocrit%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%dna%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%hiv%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%critical%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%A.%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%homocys%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%alpha%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%alcohol%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%amphetamine%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%amino%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%phago%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%gonorr%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%criteria%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%trig%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%catecholam%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%catheter%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%ch[0-9]%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%2hr%glu%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%chlamydia%' and 
	LabChemTestName not like '%chlor%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%chromi%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%LDH%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%LDL%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%FSH%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%lymph%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%meth%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%MLH1%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%thyro%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%thalas%'

into #tempHematocrit

from Src.Chem_LabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE (LabChemTestName like '%hematocrit%' OR 
	LabChemTestName like '%hem%crit%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%packed%cell%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%PCV%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%VPRC%' OR 
	LabChemTestName like '%EVF%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%erythrocy%vol%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%haemto%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%hct%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%crit%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%h%&%h%')
	AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%serotype%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%PCV [0-9]%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%HPCV%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%cryocrit%' AND

/* Hemoglobin */
/* Check for the labchemtestname in the dimension table */
select distinct labchemtestname from CDWWork.Dim.Labchemtest 
WHERE 
   (labchemtestname like '%hemoglob%' or 
    labchemtestname like '%HB%' or 
	LabChemTestName like '%HGB%') and
	labchemtestname not like 'z%' AND
	labchemtestname not like 'x%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%' AND 
	LabChemTestName not like '%HBV%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%Anti-HB%' AND 
	LabChemTestName not like '%Hepatitis%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%ELECTROPHORESIS%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%HBSAG%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%HC1 ESTERASE%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%Testoster%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%SDHB%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%HBsAb%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%GHB%' AND
	LabChemTestName NOT LIKE '%A1C%'
	AND LabChemTestName NOT LIKE '%CELL%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%RATE%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%/100wbc%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%sperm%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%HBV%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hbc%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hiv%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%anti%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hep%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%butyl%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%VITROS%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hormone%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hbs%'

select distinct b.*, a.LabChemResultValue,c.LabChemTestName, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue

into #tempHemoglobin

from Src.Chem_LabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE 
   (labchemtestname like '%hemoglob%' or 
    labchemtestname like '%HB%' or

select distinct b.*, a.LabChemResultValue,c.LabChemTestName, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue

from Src.Chem_LabChem_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE 
   ( 
    labchemtestname like '%terminal%pep%' or
	labchemtestname  like '%proBNP%' or 
	labchemtestname  like '%pro[- /_]BNP%' or 
	labchemtestname  like '%pro brain natriuretic peptide%' ) and
	LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%'

union all

select distinct b.*, a.LabChemResultValue,c.LabChemTestName, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, a.LabChemResultNumericValue

from Src.Chem_PatientLabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE 
   (
    labchemtestname like '%terminal%pep%' or
	labchemtestname  like '%proBNP%' or 
	labchemtestname  like '%pro[- /_]BNP%' or 
	labchemtestname  like '%pro brain natriuretic peptide%' ) and
	LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%'

select *
into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_PBNP
from #tempPBNP
where LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, DPP4Filltime)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_PBNP -- 378

/* Blood Pressure */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric, a.diastolic, a.Systolic,
 b.DPP4Filltime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BP

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.DPP4Filltime)) AND
c.VitalType in ('%Blood pressure%')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,a.diastolic, a.Systolic,
b.DPP4Filltime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime))AND 
c.VitalType like ('%Blood pressure%')

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BP --331

/* Total Number of HF hospitalization prior to DPP4GLP1 initiation */
drop table Dflt.HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_PriorHFHospitalization

select distinct a.*, b.AdmitDateTime

into Dflt.HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_PriorHFHospi

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('398.91','428.0','428.1','428.20','428.21','428.22','428.23','428.30',
						'428.31','428.32','428.33','428.40','428.41','428.42','428.43','428.9')
						and b.AdmitDateTime < a.DPP4Filltime
					and b.AdmitDateTime between dateadd(year, -1, a.DPP4Filltime) and a.DPP4Filltime

union all

select distinct a.*, b.AdmitDateTime

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('I50.00','I50.1','I50.20','I50.30','I50.40','I50.9',
									'I11.0','I13.0','I13.2','I25.5')
									and b.AdmitDateTime < a.DPP4Filltime
and b.AdmitDateTime between dateadd(year, -1, a.DPP4Filltime) and a.DPP4Filltime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_PriorHFHospi --168

//** After the drug initiation - Extraction **//

/* All HF hospitalization after DPP4GLP1 initiation */

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime,c.ICD9Code,b.AdmitDiagnosis
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_HFHospitalization

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('398.91','428.0','428.1','428.20','428.21','428.22','428.23','428.30',
						'428.31','428.32','428.33','428.40','428.41','428.42','428.43','428.9')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

/*ICD10 Codes*/
select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10Code, b.AdmitDiagnosis

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('I50.00','I50.1','I50.20','I50.30','I50.40','I50.9',
									'I11.0','I13.0','I13.2','I25.5')
and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_HFHospitalization --563

/* All hospitalization after DPP4GLP1 initiation */

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime,c.ICD9Code
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_AllHospitalization

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where b.DischargeDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD9Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientDiagnosis_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where b.DischargeDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD9Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientFeeDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where b.DischargeDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*, b.PatientTransferDateTime, c.ICD9Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_PatientTransferDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where b.PatientTransferDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*, b.SpecialtyTransferDateTime, c.ICD9Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_SpecialtyTransferDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where b.SpecialtyTransferDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

select distinct a.*, b.AdmitDateTime, c.ICD9Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatDischargeDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where b.AdmitDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

/*ICD10 Codes*/

/*BMI After*/
/* Select height and weight values from vital signs view to calculate bmi */
drop table #tempDiabetesVitals

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.DPP4Filltime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into #tempDiabetesVitalsDPP4GLP1

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(month, 3, b.DPP4Filltime) AND
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.DPP4Filltime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(month, 3, b.DPP4Filltime) AND 
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.DPP4Filltime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime >= b.DPP4Filltime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.DPP4Filltime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime >= b.DPP4Filltime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')

select distinct scrssn from #tempDiabetesVitalsDPP4GLP1--2278

/* Seperate weight records into a table */
select * 

into #tempweightDPP4GLP1After

from #tempDiabetesVitalsDPP4GLP1 where vitaltype = 'WEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightDPP4GLP1After--2235

/* Seperate height records into a table */
select *  

into #tempheightDPP4GLP1After

from #tempDiabetesVitalsDPP4GLP1 where vitaltype = 'HEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightDPP4GLP1After --2137

/* Combine data from table weight and height to obtain the bmi for individual patient */

select distinct a.patientsid, a.ScrSSN, cast(a.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as HeightTime, a.VitalResultNumeric as heightresult, 
cast(b.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as WeightTime,b.VitalResultNumeric as weightresult, ((b.VitalResultNumeric*703)/(a.VitalResultNumeric*a.VitalResultNumeric)) as bmi

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After

from #tempheightDPP4GLP1After as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempweightDPP4GLP1After as b with (NOLOCK) on a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime=b.VitalSignTakenDateTime 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After --2106

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After where bmi between 30 and 35 --951

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After where bmi between 35 and 40 --710

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After where bmi > 40 --466

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After where bmi < 30 --1058

select *

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMIAf

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After

		LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Olmesartan%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%telmisartan%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%irbesartan%' 

select distinct *

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_ARB1

from #medicationswithoutrefilldpp4 where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%valsartan%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%losartan%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%candesartan%' or
		LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Olmesartan%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%telmisartan%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%irbesartan%' 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_ARB1--759

/* Sacubitril and Valsartan */

select distinct * 

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_SacuVal1

from #medicationswithoutrefilldpp4
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%sacubitril%' and LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%valsartan%' 

Select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_SacuVal1 --173

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_ARB1
union all

select distinct *

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_SacuVal1

select distinct *

/* GLP1 */

/*Making sure the ICD9 and ICD10 codes relevant to VT are present in the Dimension table under CDWWork*/

select distinct ICD9Code from CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 where ICD9Code in ('427.1')

select distinct ICD10Code from CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 where ICD10Code in ('I47.0','I47.2','I47.20','I47.21','I47.29')

select distinct a.patientsid, a.scrssn, a.GLP1Filltime, b.DischargeDateTime as comorbidity_Dischargedatetime, c.ICD9Code as comorbidity_ICD9Code
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_VT

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('427.1')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >=a.GLP1Filltime
union all

select distinct a.patientsid, a.scrssn, a.GLP1Filltime, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD9Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join

CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('427.1')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >=a.GLP1Filltime
union all

/* Heart Rate 6 months before drug initiation- GLP1  */

select distinct vitaltype from CDWWork.Dim.VitalType

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric, a.systolic, a.diastolic,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_HR

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.GLP1FillTime)) AND
c.VitalType in ('pulse','zzpulse')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,a.systolic, a.diastolic,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime))AND 
c.VitalType in ('pulse','zzpulse')

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_HR --1437

/*  Heart Rate 6 months before drug initiation- DPP4 */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric, a.diastolic, a.Systolic,
 b.DPP4Filltime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_HR

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.DPP4Filltime)) AND
c.VitalType in ('pulse','zzpulse')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,a.diastolic, a.Systolic,
b.DPP4Filltime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime))AND 
c.VitalType in ('pulse','zzpulse')

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_HR --2289

/* Heart Rate 7 days after drug initiation - GLP1 */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric, a.systolic, a.diastolic,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_HRAfter

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(DAY, 7, b.GLP1FillTime) and
c.VitalType in ('pulse','zzpulse')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,a.systolic, a.diastolic,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(DAY, 7, b.GLP1FillTime) AND 
c.VitalType in ('pulse','zzpulse')

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1_HRAfter --1459

/*  Heart Rate 7 days after drug initiation- DPP4 */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric, a.diastolic, a.Systolic,
 b.DPP4Filltime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_HRAft

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(DAY, 7, b.DPP4FillTime) AND
c.VitalType in ('pulse','zzpulse')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,a.diastolic, a.Systolic,
b.DPP4Filltime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(DAY, 7, b.DPP4FillTime) AND
c.VitalType in ('pulse','zzpulse')

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1_HRAft --2320

/* LVEF Values */

Select distinct a.* ,  b.Low_Value, b.ValueDateTime, b.High_Value

into #tempefhfref

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.VINCI_TIU_NLP_LVEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where b.ValueDateTime between dateadd(Year, -1, a.glp1filltime) AND dateadd(month, 1, a.glp1filltime)

Select distinct a.* ,  b.Low_Value, b.ValueDateTime, b.High_Value

into #tempefhfrefdpp4

from Dflt.Diabetes_HFrEF_DPP4GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.VINCI_TIU_NLP_LVEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where b.ValueDateTime between dateadd(Year, -1, a.dpp4filltime) AND dateadd(month, 1, a.dpp4filltime)
